    // Indices of connected elements\n    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection\n};\n\n// Dynamic state for each element\nstruct ElementDynamic {\n    val_t current_energy;\n    val_t total_flux;\n};\n\n// World state (distributed across MPI ranks)\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    std::vector<ElementStatic> elements_static;\n    std::vector<val_t> energy;\n    std::vector<val_t> energy_swap;\n    std::vector<val_t> flux;\n    std::vector<val_t> flux_swap;\n    int n_root = 0;\n    int local_nx = 0;\n    int start_x = 0;\n    int end_x = 0;\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\nconstexpr int TAG_TO_PREV = 10;\nconstexpr int TAG_TO_NEXT = 20;\n\n// Build the local part of a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh.\n// Each rank owns a contiguous block of rows in the x direction, while all\n// ranks store the full y dimension. world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n\n    const size_t ext_rows = static_cast<size_t>(local_nx + 2);\n    world.energy.assign(ext_rows * N, 0.0);\n    world.energy_swap.assign(ext_rows * N, 0.0);\n world.elements_static.resize(local_elems);\n\n    // Initialize all local elements with default material and no connections\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < local_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n    }\n\n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n\n    for (int lx = 0; lx < local_nx; ++lx) {\n        const int x = start_x + lx;\n        for (int y = 0; y < N; ++y) {\n            const size_t idx = static_cast<size_t>(lx) * N + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n\n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < N && ny >= 0 && ny < N) {\n elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = N - 1;\n    for (int lx = 0; lx < local_nx; ++lx) {\n        const int x = start_x + lx;\n        if (x == 0) {\n            world.elements_static[static_cast<size_t>(lx) * N + 0].material_idx =\n                INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n            world.elements_static[static_cast<size_t>(lx) * N + last].material_idx =\n                OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n        } else if (x == last) {\n            world.elements_static[static_cast<size_t>(lx) * N + 0].material_idx =\n                OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n            world.elements_static[static_cast<size_t>(lx) * N + last].material_idx =\n                INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\ninline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, val_t this_energy,\n                        val_t connection_flux, val_t other_energy) {\n    return (other_energy - this_energy) *\n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Exchange the newly computed boundary rows with neighboring ranks.\nvoid exchangeHalos(World& world, const int prev_rank, const int next_rank) {\n    const int N = world.n_root;\n    const int count = N;\n\n    val_t* const data = world.energy_swap.data();\n    val_t* const halo_left = data;\n    val_t* const first_owned = data + static_cast<size_t>(1) * N;\n    val_t* const last_owned =\n        data + static_cast<size_t>(world.local_nx) * N;\n    val_t* const halo_right =\n        data + static_cast<size_t>(world.local_nx + 1) * N;\n\n    if (prev_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        MPI_Sendrecv(first_owned, count, MPI_DOUBLE, prev_rank, TAG_TO_PREV,\n                     halo_left, count, MPI_DOUBLE, prev_rank, TAG_TO_NEXT,\n                     MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n    }\n\n    if (next_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        MPI_Sendrecv(last_owned, count, MPI_DOUBLE, next_rank, TAG_TO_NEXT,\n                     halo_right, count, MPI_DOUBLE, next_rank, TAG_TO_PREV,\n                     MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n    }\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters, const int prev_rank,\n                   const int next_rank) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    const size_t row_offset = static_cast<size_t>(world.n_root);\n\n    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n        // Update all locally owned elements\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n            const ElementStatic& elem_static = world.elements_static[i];\n            const size_t dyn_idx = i + row_offset;\n            const val_t this_energy = world.energy[dyn_idx];\n            const Material& mat = world.materials[elem_static.material_idx];\n\n            // Start with external flow\n            val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n\n            // Add flux from all connected elements\n            for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n                const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n                total_flux += computeFlux(mat, this_energy,\n                                          elem_static.connected_flux[j],\n                                          world.energy[neighbor_idx]);\n            }\n\n            // Update element state\n            world.energy_swap[dyn_idx] = this_energy + total_flux;\n            world.flux_swap[dyn_idx] = world.flux[dyn_idx] + std::abs(total_flux);\n        }\n\n        // Halo values are only needed by a subsequent iteration\n        if (iter + 1 < n_iters) {\n            exchangeHalos(world, prev_rank, next_rank);\n        }\n\n        // Swap buffers\n        std::swap(world.energy, world.energy_swap);\n        std::swap(world.flux, world.flux_swap);\n    }\n}\n\n// Gather all owned element data onto rank 0 in global (x, y) order.\nvoid gatherResults(const World& world, std::vector<val_t>& all_energy,\n                   std::vector<val_t>& all_flux, const int rank,\n                   const int size) {\n    const int N = world.n_root;\n    const int local_count = world.local_nx * N;\n\n    std::vector<int> counts(size);\n    std::vector<int> displs(size);\n\n    int offset = 0;\n    for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n        const int s = static_cast<int>(static_cast<long long>(r) * N / size);\n        const int e =\n            static_cast<int>(static_cast<long long>(r + 1) * N / size);\n        counts[r] = (e - s) * N;\n        displs[r] = offset;\n        offset += counts[r];\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        all_energy.resize(static_cast<size_t>(offset));\n        all_flux.resize(static_cast<size_t>(offset));\n    }\n\n    const val_t* send_energy = world.energy.data() + N;\n    const val_t* send_flux = world.flux.data() + N;\n    val_t* recv_energy = (rank == 0) ? all_energy.data() : nullptr;\n    val_t* recv_flux = (rank == 0) ? all_flux.data() : nullptr;\n\n    MPI_Gatherv(send_energy, local_count, MPI_DOUBLE, recv_energy,\n                counts.data(), displs.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    MPI_Gatherv(send_flux, local_count, MPI_DOUBLE, recv_flux, counts.data(),\n                displs.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const std::vector<val_t>& energy,\n                     const std::vector<val_t>& flux) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n this can happen with external flows\n    }\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<val_t>& energy,\n                     const std::vector<val_t>& flux) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < energy.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr =\n            reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&energy[i]);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&flux[i]);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>
